=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given integers N and M.
Print all integer sequences (A_1, A_2, \ldots, A_N) of length N that satisfy all of the following conditions, in lexicographical order.

- 1 \leq A_i
- A_{i - 1} + 10 \leq A_i for each integer i from 2 through N
- A_N \leq M

What is lexicographical order?
A sequence S = (S_1, S_2, \ldots, S_N) of length N is smaller in lexicographical order than a sequence T = (T_1, T_2, \ldots, T_N) of length N if and only if there exists an integer 1 \leq i \leq N such that both of the following hold:

-  (S_1, S_2, \ldots, S_{i-1}) = (T_1, T_2, \ldots, T_{i-1})
-  S_i is less than T_i (as a number).

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M

Output

Let X be the number of integer sequences that satisfy the conditions, and print X + 1 lines.
The first line should contain the value of X.
The (i + 1)-th line (1 \leq i \leq X) should contain the i-th smallest integer sequence in lexicographical order, with elements separated by spaces.

Constraints


- 2 \leq N \leq 12
- 10N - 9 \leq M \leq 10N
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

3 23

Sample Output 1

10
1 11 21
1 11 22
1 11 23
1 12 22
1 12 23
1 13 23
2 12 22
2 12 23
2 13 23
3 13 23

(1, 11, 21), (1, 11, 22), (1, 11, 23), (1, 12, 22), (1, 12, 23), (1, 13, 23), (2, 12, 22), (2, 12, 23), (2, 13, 23), (3, 13, 23) are the 10 sequences that satisfy the conditions.

Use the following starter code to write the final solution.
```python

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

import sys
def main():
    N,M=map(int,sys.stdin.readline().split())
    K = M - 10*(N-1)
    from math import comb
    X = comb(N+K-1, N)
    out = [str(X)]
    arr = []
    def dfs(pos, last):
        if pos == N:
            out.append(' '.join(str(arr[i] + 10*i) for i in range(N)))
            return
        start = last if pos > 0 else 1
        for v in range(start, K+1):
            arr.append(v)
            dfs(pos+1, v)
            arr.pop()
    dfs(0,1)
    sys.stdout.write('\n'.join(out))

Count of nondecreasing sequences length N from 1..K equals C(N+K-1,N). We can compute:

def count_sequences(n,k):
    res=1
    for i in range(1,n+1):
        res = res * (k + i - 1) // i
    return res

Because product (k)(k+1)...(k+n-1)/n! = C(k+n-1,n). For k=3,n=3: res=1; i=1 res=3; i=2 res=3*4//2=6; i=3 res=6*5//3=10. For k=1,n=12: i=1 res=1; i=2 res=1*2//2=1; ... res=1. Good. import sys

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    seqs = []
    arr = []

    def dfs(pos):
        if pos == N:
            seqs.append(" ".join(map(str, arr)))
            return
        start = 1 if pos == 0 else arr[-1] + 10
        end = M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)
        for v in range(start, end + 1):
            arr.append(v)
            dfs(pos + 1)
            arr.pop()

    dfs(0)
    sys.stdout.write(str(len(seqs)) + "\n" + "\n".join(seqs))

Let's choose a clean code:

import sys

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    K = M - 10 * (N - 1)

    # number of nondecreasing B sequences = C(N+K-1, N)
    x = 1
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        x = x * (K + i - 1) // i

    out = [str(x)]
    arr = []

    def dfs(pos):
        if pos == N:
            out.append(" ".join(map(str, arr)))
            return
        start = 1 if pos == 0 else arr[-1] + 10
        end = M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)
        for v in range(start, end + 1):
            arr.append(v)
            dfs(pos + 1)
            arr.pop()

    dfs(0)
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    out = []

        if pos == N:

import sys

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    ans = []
    cur = []

    def dfs(pos):
        if pos == N:
            ans.append(" ".join(map(str, cur)))
            return
        low = 1 if pos == 0 else cur[-1] + 10
        high = M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)
        for v in range(low, high + 1):
            cur.append(v)
            dfs(pos + 1)
            cur.pop()

    dfs(0)
    ans.insert(0, str(len(ans)))
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Streaming code:

import sys

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    K = M - 10 * (N - 1)

    x = 1
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        x = x * (K + i - 1) // i

    write = sys.stdout.write
    write(str(x) + "\n")

    buf = []
    cur = []

    def dfs(pos):
        if pos == N:
            buf.append(" ".join(map(str, cur)))
            if len(buf) >= 10000:
                write("\n".join(buf) + "\n")
                buf.clear()
            return
        low = 1 if pos == 0 else cur[-1] + 10
        high = M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)
        for v in range(low, high + 1):
            cur.append(v)
            dfs(pos + 1)
            cur.pop()

    dfs(0)
    if buf:
        write("\n".join(buf) + "\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

This is memory efficient and still simple. It uses count formula. Should we use this final? It is a bit more code but not much. The problem constraints output max 293k lines, so list version is fine. List version:
```
import sys
def main():
    N,M=map(int,sys.stdin.readline().split())
    ans=[]
    cur=[]
    def dfs(pos):
        if pos==N:
            ans.append(" ".join(map(str,cur)))
            return
        low=1 if pos==0 else cur[-1]+10
        high=M-10*(N-1-pos)
        for v in range(low, high+1):
            cur.append(v)
            dfs(pos+1)
            cur.pop()
    dfs(0)
    ans.insert(0, str(len(ans)))
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(ans))
if __name__=="__main__": main()
```
Streaming version:
```
import sys
def main():
    N,M=map(int,sys.stdin.readline().split())
    K=M-10*(N-1)
    x=1
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        x=x*(K+i-1)//i
    write=sys.stdout.write
    write(str(x)+"\n")
    buf=[]
    cur=[]
    def dfs(pos):
        if pos==N:
            buf.append(" ".join(map(str,cur)))
            if len(buf)>=10000:
                write("\n".join(buf))
                write("\n")
                buf.clear()
            return
        low=1 if pos==0 else cur[-1]+10
        high=M-10*(N-1-pos)
        for v in range(low, high+1):
            cur.append(v)
            dfs(pos+1)
            cur.pop()
    dfs(0)
    if buf:
        write("\n".join(buf))
        write("\n")
if __name__=="__main__": main()
```

Are we sure every valid A sequence corresponds to a nondecreasing B sequence with values 1..K? Yes. Let's re-derive: Let B_i = A_i - 10(i-1). Then B_1 = A_1 >=1. B_i - B_{i-1} = A_i - A_{i-1} - 10 >=0. B_N = A_N - 10(N-1) <= M - 10(N-1) = K. Conversely, any nondecreasing B in [1,K] gives A_i = B_i + 10(i-1). Then A_i - A_{i-1} = B_i - B_{i-1} + 10 >=10, A_N = B_N + 10(N-1) <= M. So bijection. Count formula correct. Good.

Let's ensure code block language is python. Final answer:
```python
import sys

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())

    K = M - 10 * (N - 1)

    # Number of nondecreasing B sequences = C(N+K-1, N)
    x = 1
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        x = x * (K + i - 1) // i

    write = sys.stdout.write
    write(str(x) + "\n")

    buf = []
    cur = []

    def dfs(pos):
        if pos == N:
            buf.append(" ".join(map(str, cur)))
            if len(buf) >= 10000:
                write("\n".join(buf))
                write("\n")
                buf.clear()
            return

        low = 1 if pos == 0 else cur[-1] + 10
        high = M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)

        for v in range(low, high + 1):
            cur.append(v)
            dfs(pos + 1)
            cur.pop()

    dfs(0)

    if buf:
        write("\n".join(buf))
        write("\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's verify with Python splitlines: `"1\nseq\n".splitlines()` -> ['1','seq']. So two lines. Good.

Good. If K negative, x=0? For K=-1,N=3: i=1 x=-1; i=2 x=-1*0//2=0; i=3 0. Count 0. But constraints no. Good.

Now, let's think if there is any issue with "M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)" if M small and pos=0 high maybe 0; range empty. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "A_i" if N=2,M=11, direct DFS:
pos0 low1 high=11-10=1 v=1
pos1 low=11 high=11 v=11. Output "1 11". Count formula K=1, x=1. Good.

Now, let's think if there is any issue with "A_i" if N=2,M=12:
pos0 low1 high=2 v=1,2
v=1 pos1 low=11 high=12 -> 11,12
v=2 pos1 low=12 high=12 -> 12
Output count 3. Lex order: (1,11),(1,12),(2,12). Good.

If we want to optimize, we could make `buf` and `write` local and use `nonlocal`? We don't reassign `buf` (we call clear), so no nonlocal needed. `write` is read. Good.

Let's verify with Python splitlines: `"1\nseq\n".splitlines()` -> ['1','seq']. So two lines. Good.

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def main():
    N, M = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())

    K = M - 10 * (N - 1)

    # X = C(N + K - 1, N)
    x = 1
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        x = x * (K + i - 1) // i

    write = sys.stdout.write
    write(str(x) + "\n")

    buf = []
    cur = []

    def dfs(pos):
        if pos == N:
            buf.append(" ".join(map(str, cur)))
            if len(buf) >= 10000:
                write("\n".join(buf))
                write("\n")
                buf.clear()
            return

        low = 1 if pos == 0 else cur[-1] + 10
        high = M - 10 * (N - 1 - pos)

        for v in range(low, high + 1):
            cur.append(v)
            dfs(pos + 1)
            cur.pop()

    dfs(0)

    if buf:
        write("\n".join(buf))
        write("\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
